- `ack_not_gated_on_sub` / ingress / handshaked `A1` with **no** subscription commits a txn / assert ack still queued (acks need only handshake).
- `ack_asset_correlator` / ingress / handshaked `A1` completes a chunked upload of asset `5` (`more=0` final frame commits) / assert `tgp;a;tok=A1;asset=5` queued (asset correlator on the chunk-commit ack, G4), and no `txn=`.
- `vp_commit_no_ack` / ingress / handshaked `A1` creates a valid pinned viewport via `tgp;vp` / assert **no** `tgp;a` is queued for it (v1 does not ack `vp` frames; the viewport's existence is observable via `viewport_count`/`viewport_pixel_rect`). Confirms the v1 ack-emission-site closure above.
- `enc_bin_probe_ack` / ingress / handshaked `A1` sends a minimal no-op `enc=bin;txn=91` patch (the probe) / assert `tgp;a;tok=A1;txn=91` queued.
- `failed_decode_no_ack` / ingress / `enc=bin` frame that fails to decode / assert `code=parse_error` queued and **no** `tgp;a` for that txn.
- `ack_unhandshaked_silent` / ingress / tokenless writer's frame that would otherwise commit / assert no `tgp;a` queued (G5).
- `backchannel_over_cap_drops_frame` / ingress / handshaked `A1`; without draining `pty_replies`, drive enough error/ack emissions that the cumulative queued bytes for `A1` reach `max_backchannel_bytes`, then trigger one more `emit_error` / assert the extra frame is **not** queued (`backchannel_pending("A1")` does not exceed `max_backchannel_bytes`) and no panic; after draining `pty_replies`, a subsequent `emit_error` queues normally (cap measured against still-pending bytes).

## P5-T26

**Title:** The fuzz oracle at `Parser::advance(&mut Term)` — single normative invariant

**Deliverable:** Add `fuzz/` target (cargo-fuzz / `libfuzzer-sys`) `fuzz_targets/tgp_ingress.rs` driving arbitrary bytes through `Parser::advance(&mut Term, bytes)` into a **GPU-free `Term`** (no wgpu device). After draining the corpus the harness asserts the four-part oracle via CPU-side accessors, holding for **any** input (adversarial, truncated, over-cap, pathological): (1) **no crash** — no panic/abort/stream-corruption; framer re-syncs on the next `tgp;` header; (2) **bounded memory at caps** — per-asset, per-session reassembly, VRAM, expanded-output all respected; (3) **bounded progress** — consume-N exits after exactly `len` bytes or the inactivity/reset abort fires (drive the injectable clock); (4) **commit / structured error / clean drop — never torn**. At quiescence `Term::pending_bytes()==0` and `Term::session_pending_bytes()==0` (no leak) and `Term::vram_used()` equals the committed scene's accounted footprint. No RGP-differential target exists (G8). Seed the corpus from the per-rule cases below. Add a deterministic seed-replay unit test so CI runs the seeds without the fuzzer.

**Design refs:** §15.5 (fuzz oracle), §15.1; G6, G8.

**Depends on:** P5-T3 (private per-token id maps — grounds the `oracle_never_torn` "cross-token write" seed, which under G11 ends in **coexistence** (a token writing its own id), **`bad_ref`** (a handle ref absent from the bearing token's map, no existence oracle) for a handshaked token, or a **clean drop** for a tokenless writer — **never `denied`** (G12.2 RESERVED), never a torn scene), P5-T6, P5-T7, P5-T8, P5-T9, P5-T10, P5-T11, P5-T12, P5-T13, P5-T14 (instance caps/VRAM footprint counted in `oracle_vram_matches_committed`), P5-T15 (per-session structural caps — the oracle's `session_pending_bytes()==0` quiescence + `max_pending_bytes_session` bound depend on T15's budget accounting), P5-T16 (viewport pixel-cap rejection — grounds `oracle_bounded_memory_at_caps` and the s15.1 oversized-viewport VRAM-exhaustion vector: an over-cap or huge-cellrect `vp` must allocate at most the screen-clamped, cap-bounded target, never an unbounded one), P5-T17, P5-T19, P5-T20, P5-T21 (lifecycle teardown — grounds the "clean drop / never torn after teardown" clause), P5-T22 (the harness exercises framer/demux/decode/caps/teardown end-to-end), P5-T23 (framing-robustness rejects — grounds clause-1 "framer re-syncs on the next `tgp;` header after any rejection": `field_order`/`enc=bin`-unadvertised enter no binary-read state, b64/trailer/trailing-bytes errors all return to Ground, which `oracle_no_crash_on_random_bytes` asserts via the re-sync check), P5-T24 (duplicate-id / OOB-accessor / semantic rejects — grounds the `oracle_never_torn` seed list's "duplicate ids" and "OOB accessor" cases, each of which must yield exactly one atomic structured `x` and leave `scene_revision` unchanged).

**Tests:**
- `oracle_no_crash_on_random_bytes` / ingress / 10k iterations of arbitrary random byte slices through `advance` / assert no panic and after each, feeding a valid `tgp;q;v=1;tok=A1` still yields a `tgp;r` (framer re-synced).
- `oracle_quiescence_no_leak` / ingress / replay every seed corpus case, then feed an empty slice + advance clock past inactivity / assert `pending_bytes()==0` and `session_pending_bytes()==0`.
- `oracle_vram_matches_committed` / ingress / replay corpus to a quiescent committed scene / assert `vram_used()` equals the sum of the committed assets'/instances'/targets' accounted footprint (recomputed independently in-test).
- `oracle_bounded_memory_at_caps` / ingress / seed: over-cap `len`, billion-element accessor, CBOR array bomb / assert peak `pending_bytes()` and `vram_used()` never exceed the advertised caps at any step.
- `oracle_bounded_progress_consume_n` / ingress / seed: truncated binary header (declared `len` > arrived bytes) / assert consume-N exits via the inactivity abort after advancing the clock (no indefinite hold), `code=truncated`.
- `oracle_never_torn` / ingress / seed: each adversarial case (truncated binary, over-cap len, duplicate ids, OOB accessor, **cross-token write** — token B naming token A's id, which under G11 lands on B's own private map: coexistence if it is a string-id upsert, `bad_ref` if it is a handle ref absent from B's map, never `denied`) / assert each ends in exactly one of {committed valid bounded scene, atomic structured `x` for a handshaked token, clean drop for a tokenless writer} and never a partial/torn scene (assert `scene_revision` either bumped-once-with-full-commit or unchanged, never mid-state). Specifically assert the cross-token-write seed never emits `denied` (RESERVED, G12.2) and never mutates A's objects (private-map isolation, G11).
- `oracle_corpus_seed_replay` / ingress / the deterministic CI replay of all seeded cases / assert the run completes within the time budget and all four oracle clauses hold for every seed.

## Review resolution log (round 1)

- **P5-R-01 (kind_conflict + cycle, ZERO tests, high):** resolved — added to P5-T24 deliverable code list, depends-on (P1 patch decode + commit-time parent-chain walk), and four concrete tests: `kind_conflict_multiple_selectors` (`code=kind_conflict;detail=multiple_selectors`, §8.6), `kind_conflict_change_kind` (change-kind reject), `cycle_self_parent` (`code=cycle;detail=a`), `cycle_two_node_loop` (two-node parent loop resolved against final staged state). Both codes now covered with byte-level inputs + accessor assertions.
- **P5-R-02 (bad_layout, ZERO tests, high):** resolved — added `bad_layout` to P5-T24's covered-code list + depends-on (P2 instancing), and two tests: `instances_xforms_len_mismatch` (`detail=xforms_len`, 9×64 bytes vs `count:10`) and `instances_tints_len_mismatch` (`detail=tints_len`, 9×4 bytes vs `count:10`), matching the §8.6 reject spelling `bad_layout;detail=xforms_len|tints_len|count_mismatch`.
- **P5-R-03 (max_viewport_px_h not independently tested, medium):** resolved — reworded `viewport_px_over_cap_rejected` to pin the width-triggered path explicitly and added `viewport_px_h_over_cap_rejected` (height over cap, width within), both asserting the shared `detail=max_viewport_px` **(superseded — see G12 table; the shared `max_viewport_px` spelling is replaced by the axis-suffixed `max_viewport_px_w`/`max_viewport_px_h` per P5-6)** while exercising the two independent enforcement axes (G7).
- **P5-R-04 (max_backchannel_bytes not enforced, medium):** resolved — added a `max_backchannel_bytes` enforcement deliverable to P5-T25 (shared queueing path for `tgp;x`/`a`/`e`, per-token, drop-new-frame on over-cap measured against still-pending `pty_replies` bytes; cited default 4096 from s07.1), new accessor `Term::backchannel_pending(tok)`, and test `backchannel_over_cap_drops_frame` (queue to cap, assert extra frame dropped, then drain and assert emission resumes). Closes the advertised-but-unenforced G7 gap inside this phase.
- **P5-R-05 (enc=bin without `bin` advertised, medium):** resolved — added to P5-T23 deliverable (§7.1 reject) + test `enc_bin_without_advertise_rejected` (harness term omits `bin`; assert `code=parse_error` and **no** binary-read state entered, trailing bytes parsed as fresh frame).
- **P5-R-06 (b64 len char-count mismatch, medium):** resolved — added to P5-T23 deliverable (§8.3 char-count validation) + test `b64_len_mismatch_rejected` (`enc=b64;len=100` with 80 actual chars → `code=parse_error`, distinct from `detail=base64` decode failure, Ground re-synced).
- **P5-R-07 (control frame inside payload consumed as payload, medium):** resolved — added test `control_frame_inside_payload_is_payload` to P5-T6 (valid `enc=bin` payload literally spelling `ESC _ tgp;q … ESC \`; assert no `tgp;r` for the embedded query, consume-N exits after exactly N bytes, surrounding patch commits). Pins the §8.2/§7.2 "not mid-binary / escape-scan disabled" invariant.
- **P5-R-08 (ack vp/asset correlators, low):** resolved — added `ack_asset_correlator` (chunk-commit ack `asset=5`, G4/§8.7) to P5-T25; for `vp` the design has no v1 positive ack-emission site (§8.6 patch-commit `txn`, §8.7 chunk `asset`, §7.1 probe `txn` are the only ack loci), so added an explicit deliverable clarification that v1 does not ack `vp` frames plus a negative test `vp_commit_no_ack`. `encode_ack` still accepts a `vp=` correlator for G4 completeness.
- **P5-R-09 (3J/2K erase paths thin, low):** resolved — added `pinned_persists_across_3j` (pinned + scene survive `CSI 3J`, §10.7) and `2k_erases_inline_row_recomputes_rect` (`CSI 2K` row-erase shrinks inline cell rect) to P5-T19.
- **P5-R-10 (viewport camera/root late-bind fallback, low):** resolved — added the G2 robustness regression to P5-T16 deliverable + new accessor `Term::viewport_uses_default_camera(vp)` + two tests: `vp_created_before_camera_no_reject` (created-before-camera renders default then silently adopts) and `camera_removed_falls_back_to_default` (`node.remove` of the camera node falls viewport back to default, never `denied`, never crash, §10.5).
- **P5-R-11 (b64 over-cap mixed with non-existent drain path, medium):** resolved — reworded P5-T6 deliverable to scope drain-then-error to `enc=bin` only and add a dedicated "b64 over-cap (no drain)" paragraph (b64 stays in-APC, bounded at `ceil(max*4/3)`, never enters binary-read; §8.2/§8.3/§7.1); reworded `b64_over_cap_decoded_size` to additionally assert no binary-read state was ever entered (bytes after the ST parsed as a fresh frame), keeping the `pending_bytes()==0` assertion as a never-nonzero check.
- **P5-R-12 (unknown_op negative-only, low):** resolved (decision made explicit) — renamed test to `unknown_op_skipped_within_version` and added an explicit reachability decision to its description: an unknown patch `do:` op is **not** an `unknown_op` trigger (§7.3 silent skip). Per the authoritative addendum (G12.3 row 8) `unknown_op` is **REACHABLE** via the P4 closed-anim-verb locus — a P4 surface, so no P5 ingress path emits it and its closed-set membership is carried by P5-T1. Negative test retained to pin the patch-op-skip path. (Supersedes the earlier "reserved" framing of this entry.)
- **P5-R-13 (unknown_node no-op-only, low):** resolved — added positive test `unknown_node_emitted` (`node.instance_set`/`node.visible` on a missing id → `code=unknown_node`, §8.6) plus an explicit reachability decision (fires on ops addressing an existing node; does NOT fire on `node.remove` of a missing id nor on `parent`/`mesh`/`material` refs, which yield `bad_ref`). Kept `unknown_node_remove_noop` (renamed-in-place) as the no-op confirmation.
- **P5-R-14 (truncated vs parse_error;detail=truncated split, medium):** resolved — added a "Taxonomy split (normative)" note to both P5-T8 (inactivity/RIS abort ⇒ top-level `code=truncated`, s15.2) and P5-T22 (PTY-EOF mid-binary + CBOR-short ⇒ `code=parse_error;detail=truncated`, s08.2/s08.3), with a cross-reference so the two adjacent scenarios assert distinct codes. Confirmed the spine intends both (G3 lists `truncated` as a top-level code; §8.2/§8.3 use it as a `parse_error` detail) — no spine change needed.
- **P5-R-15 (fuzz-oracle depends-on under-specified, low):** resolved — added P5-T14 (instance caps/VRAM footprint), P5-T15 (per-session structural caps incl. `max_pending_bytes_session` / `session_pending_bytes`), and the teardown tasks P5-T19/T20/T21 to P5-T26's depends-on, each annotated with the oracle clause it grounds.

## Review resolution log (round 2)

- **P5-1 (DECSTR-aborts-in-flight-binary asserted but ZERO test, medium):** resolved — added the lifecycle test `decstr_aborts_inflight_binary_emits_truncated` to P5-T8: handshake `A1`, feed `tgp;p;tok=A1;txn=61;enc=bin;len=1000` + 300 bytes, then `ESC [ ! p` (DECSTR); assert `code=truncated` with `txn=61` queued to the still-live `A1`, `pending_bytes()==0`, and committed scene/token survive (`token_handshaked("A1")==true`, `scene_is_empty()` unchanged). Cited s15.2 (abort emits top-level `truncated`) + s10.7 (DECSTR leaves the committed scene/handshake intact, so the *uncommitted* in-flight frame's abort is reported to the live token). This is the DECSTR companion to T17's RIS-abort test, pinning the per-reset observable.
- **P5-2 (`anim_speed_max` / `camera_report_hz` advertised-but-unowned, medium):** resolved (scoped-out with explicit ownership) — added a **Cap-ownership scope** note to the P5 preamble that narrows P5's "enforces every advertised cap before allocation" claim to the allocation/framing/structural/sizing caps it owns, and names the four behavioral/negotiation caps as OOS for P5's caps tasks with their owning phase: `anim_speed_max` → P4 anim playback (`anim;play;speed>cap` clamp/reject, confirmed by s14.4), `camera_report_hz` → P2/P4 explore camera-report throttle (G6 injectable clock), and `msaa`/`click_deadzone_px` → P3/P2 render-input options with no reject path. Added a cross-ref clause to P5-T15's deliverable so a reader looking for per-session caps there is pointed to the scope note. This closes the G7 hole (advertised==enforced stays whole across the plan) without P5 double-owning behavioral clamps; their enforcement tests live in the owning phase per the design (s14.4 makes `anim_speed_max` an `anim` reject, not a P5 surface).
- **P5-3 (T8↔T17 contradiction: does RIS abort emit `truncated`?, medium):** resolved — reworded P5-T8's taxonomy note and added an **"Emission is gated per-reset (normative)"** paragraph: all three abort triggers (inactivity/stall, DECSTR, RIS) free the buffer identically, but inactivity + DECSTR emit top-level `code=truncated` to the still-live handshaked token while **RIS suppresses the emission** because RIS invalidates all tokens (G10/§10.7) and G1/G5 gating drops the frame — exactly what T17 `ris_aborts_inflight_binary` asserts. T8 no longer claims "RIS abort emits truncated"; the abort routes through `should_emit` (P5-T2), which naturally suppresses under RIS. T8 and T17 now agree on the (different) observable for each reset.
- **P5-4 (`node_busy_emitted` vague/mis-categorized placeholder, medium):** resolved — rewrote it as the concrete **ingress** test `node_busy_upsert_trs_on_played_node` grounded in s14.5: handshake `A1` (anim feat), commit `node.upsert{id:"arm", trs:…}` + a clip-bearing GLB (commit ack maps animation-index → `ClipId c`), `anim;play;clip=c;node="arm"`, then a new-txn `node.upsert{id:"arm", trs:{t:[1,0,0]}}` writing an animated TRS field of the played node / assert `code=node_busy`, whole txn rejected, `"arm"` local TRS unchanged. Added the s14.5 boundary assertion that a non-TRS upsert (`visible:false`) on the same played node does **not** fire `node_busy`. Re-categorized from `interaction` to `ingress` (the trigger is a `node.upsert` patch, not a pointer event). Also corrected T24's depends-on: `node_busy` moved from "P2 (sub/explore)" to "P4 (anim …)" since the played-node TRS-write reject is owned by P4 anim playback.
- **P5-5 (fuzz-oracle T26 depends-on still incomplete, medium):** resolved — added P5-T3, P5-T16, P5-T23, P5-T24 to P5-T26's depends-on, each annotated with the oracle clause/seed it grounds (mirroring the P5-R-15 style): T3 grounds the `oracle_never_torn` "cross-token write" seed (→ `denied`/clean-drop); T16 grounds `oracle_bounded_memory_at_caps` + the s15.1 oversized-viewport VRAM vector (screen-clamped, cap-bounded target); T23 grounds clause-1 framer re-sync after framing rejects (`oracle_no_crash_on_random_bytes`); T24 grounds the `oracle_never_torn` "duplicate ids" + "OOB accessor" seeds (one atomic `x`, `scene_revision` unchanged).
- **P5-6 (`detail=max_viewport_px` ≠ advertised axis names, low):** resolved (option a) — changed P5-T16 to emit the **axis-specific advertised** detail names `max_viewport_px_w` / `max_viewport_px_h` (instead of the unsuffixed `max_viewport_px`), satisfying the s15.2 "same field name in reply, enforcement, and `detail=`" rule with s07.1's two advertised caps, and added a **`detail=` spelling note** to the deliverable flagging the inherited s10.3/G7 (unsuffixed) vs s07.1/s15.2 (suffixed) ambiguity and resolving it for G7 uniformity (detail is non-load-bearing, so no wire-compat break). Updated both pixel-cap tests (`viewport_px_over_cap_rejected` → `detail=max_viewport_px_w`, `viewport_px_h_over_cap_rejected` → `detail=max_viewport_px_h`). (The round-1 log entry P5-R-03 still records the old shared spelling; it is left as a historical record and is superseded by this entry.)
- **P5-7 (T11/T14 double-claim `max_instances_per_buffer`, low):** resolved — removed `max_instances_per_buffer` from P5-T11's deliverable cap list and depends-on (which had a hedging "instance-count cap depends on P2-T*" note), making P5-T14 the single owner of instance-count enforcement and `detail=max_instances`. Added an explicit one-clause deferral in T11's deliverable so the cap→task mapping is 1:1 and no implementer wires instance-count enforcement twice. No coverage change (T14 already tests it).
- **P5-8 (`null_not_allowed` second locus untested, low):** resolved — added `null_not_allowed_on_kind_selector` to P5-T24: `node.upsert{id:"n", mesh:<CBOR null>}` (kind selector present-but-null) / assert `code=null_not_allowed;detail=mesh`, whole txn rejected, node not created. Pins the §8.6 second non-nullable locus (kind selector) distinct from the existing `id`-locus test. The `code` was already covered; this closes the documented dual-locus gap for defense-in-depth.

## Cleanup resolution log (pass 1)

This pass applies the round-3 open-issue review (`P5.review.md`, I-1..I-6), the authoritative reconciliation addendum (`tgp-reconciliation-addendum.md`, G11/G12), and the explicit DEDUP/alias duties for this phase. The four high/medium review issues (I-1/I-2/I-3/I-6) and the G11 addendum are the *same* underlying ruling — the private per-token id map makes cross-token addressing unrepresentable, so `denied` is RESERVED — and are resolved together in the P5-T3 rewrite.

### Open issues (P5.review.md I-1..I-6)

- **I-1 (medium — cross-token camera ref must be late-bind, not `denied`):** resolved. P5-T3's old `cross_token_viewport_camera_ref_denied` test is rewritten as `cross_token_viewport_camera_late_binds_default`: B2's `tgp;vp;camera="cam"` (where `"cam"` lives only in A1's private map) is **created with the implicit default camera** (`viewport_uses_default_camera(vp)==true`), no `denied`/`bad_ref` — indistinguishable from create-before-camera (G2/G11(c)). The T3 deliverable's resolver-routing bullet now routes viewport `camera`/`root` through the late-bind path, never `denied`.
- **I-2 (medium — cross-token mesh/handle ref must be `bad_ref`, no existence leak):** resolved. P5-T3's old `cross_token_mesh_ref_denied` is rewritten as `cross_token_mesh_ref_bad_ref_no_leak` asserting `code=bad_ref;detail=mesh:7`, **byte-for-byte identical** to the never-created case (no existence oracle, G11(b)), consistent with P5-T10 `inflight_id_not_yet_committed`. Added `private_maps_no_existence_oracle` to pin that `bad_ref` for "absent in my map" vs "absent everywhere" differ only in the literal id, so a probing token cannot enumerate another token's handle space.
- **I-3 (medium — deliverable over-broadly routes every ref through `resolve_owned → denied`):** resolved. P5-T3's deliverable is rewritten: `resolve_owned` is now `-> Option<&Obj>` (an ordinary in-namespace resolver, not a cross-token gate), and a per-kind "Resolver routing" section narrows behaviors — string node-id upsert/remove index only the requester's own map (coexistence/local-no-op), handle refs → `bad_ref`, viewport camera/root → late-bind default. The `denied`-on-any-cross-token-miss prose (root cause of I-1/I-2/I-6) is removed.
- **I-4 (low — configurable advertised-set test seam unnamed):** resolved. Named the single seam `Term::new_with_caps(caps: AdvertisedCaps)` (builder over the frozen v1 advertised cap/enc/feat set) + `&self` accessor `Term::advertised_caps()`, **owned by P5-T11** and **consumed by P5-T23**. Updated `unadvertised_cap_not_enforced` (T11) and `enc_bin_without_advertise_rejected` (T23) to build via the seam and assert the omission via `advertised_caps()` before feeding the breaching bytes.
- **I-5 (low — `oversized_cellrect_allocates_screen_clamped` mislabeled `lifecycle`):** resolved. Relabeled the test `lifecycle`→`ingress` (bytes-in, CPU pixel-rect accessor out; no RIS/DECSTR/resize/EOF drive), with an inline note citing I-5.
- **I-6 (high — `cross_token_upsert_denied` vs `same_id_two_tokens_no_collision` self-contradictory; `denied` unreachable under private maps):** resolved via the review's recommended **branch (a): `denied` is v1-unreachable / RESERVED** (consistent with the `(TokenId,Id)` private-map deliverable and ratified by G11(a)). P5-T3 now asserts **coexistence** for the same-id case (`same_id_two_tokens_coexist`) and rewrites the contradictory denial tests: `cross_token_upsert_denied` → `cross_token_upsert_creates_own_node_not_denied` (B2 gets its own node, A1's unchanged) and `cross_token_remove_denied` → `cross_token_remove_is_local_noop` (remove of an id absent from B2's map is the §8.6 no-op). `denied`'s closed-set membership is carried solely by P5-T1's `error_code_wire_strings_closed_set` + `reserved_codes_round_trip_only` (mirroring `msg_too_large`, the other genuinely RESERVED code — note `unknown_op` is NOT reserved: it is reachable via the P4 closed-anim-verb locus, G12.3 row 8), so no coverage is lost while the two contradictory outcomes for the identical op shape are removed.

### Addendum G11 (private per-token isolation; `denied` RESERVED)

- **G11(a) — rewrite any `denied`-on-cross-token-collision test:** resolved. All five old `denied`-asserting cross-token tests in P5-T3 are rewritten to their G11 outcomes (coexistence / local-no-op / `bad_ref` / late-bind default / silent drop). The deliverable now states cross-token addressing is unrepresentable by construction and `denied` has no reachable emitter, with the no-side-channel rule (G11(b′)): physically-disjoint per-token maps, a single global id table is non-conforming.
- **G11(b) — cross-token mesh/handle ref → `bad_ref`, no existence leak:** resolved (see I-2). `cross_token_mesh_ref_bad_ref_no_leak` + `private_maps_no_existence_oracle`.
- **G11(c) — cross-token camera/root ref → late-bind default:** resolved (see I-1). `cross_token_viewport_camera_late_binds_default`; T3 deliverable routes camera/root through late-bind. The in-token create-before-camera + remove-fallback regressions remain in P5-T16 (`vp_created_before_camera_no_reject`, `camera_removed_falls_back_to_default`).
- **G11(d) — no-handshake token-requiring frame → `bad_token`; well-formed tokenless `tgp;p` → silent drop:** resolved. P5-T4 already handles handshake `bad_token` (malformed/unacceptable token); P5-T3's `tokenless_frame_dropped_silently` pins that a well-formed tokenless `tgp;p` is dropped silently (not `denied`, not `bad_token`), per G5/G11(d).

### Addendum G12 (error-code reachability; emissions match the table)

- **G12.1 — over-`max_msg_mb` → `cap_exceeded;detail=max_msg` (not `msg_too_large`):** resolved. P5-T6 deliverable + tests (`over_cap_bin_drains_and_errors`, `b64_over_cap_decoded_size`, `drain_only_allocates_nothing`) now emit/assert `code=cap_exceeded;detail=max_msg` on both the `enc=bin` drain locus and the b64/decode-size locus; added a "Canonical code" paragraph reading the s15.2/s15.3 `msg_too_large` loci as `cap_exceeded;detail=max_msg`. P5-T7 `below_ceil_above_msg_drains` and P5-T1 `emit_error_op_omitted_when_unknown` updated off `msg_too_large`. **Note on the over-max_msg_mb ruling:** the duty's phrase "over-max_msg_mb → cap_exceeded" is applied exactly as G12.1 spells it: `detail=max_msg` (the per-message member of the `cap_exceeded` family), distinct from `detail=unrecoverable` (the `len > BIN_HARD_CEIL` hard-ceil variant, P5-T7) — both `cap_exceeded`, separated by `detail=`.
- **G12.2/G12.3 — reachable/reserved partition (24 reachable, 2 reserved: `msg_too_large`, `denied`):** resolved per the authoritative addendum. `unknown_op` is **REACHABLE** via the P4 closed-anim-verb locus (G12.3 row 8) — NOT reserved — but has no P5 ingress emitter. P5-T1 adds `reserved_codes_round_trip_only` asserting the 2 reserved codes are closed-set round-trip only with no reachable emitter anywhere, and round-trips `unknown_op` as a reachable-but-no-P5-emitter code; the deliverable + preamble state the 24+2 partition. P5-T24's deliverable is rescoped to "every REACHABLE G3 code (the 24)" and notes `unknown_op` reachable-in-P4 (P5 patch-op-skip negative-test only), `denied` reserved (carried by T1). No P5 ingress test emits either of the 2 reserved codes or `unknown_op`.
- **G12.4 — `instanced_node_tint` → `kind_conflict;detail=instanced_node_tint` (struck `bad_param` synonym):** resolved. P5-T24 test renamed `instanced_node_tint_bad_param` → `instanced_node_tint_kind_conflict` asserting `code=kind_conflict;detail=instanced_node_tint`; deliverable code list + depends-on updated (the trigger moves from `bad_param` to `kind_conflict`). `bad_param`'s coverage is retained via P5-T5's `node_id_*_rejected` (charset/too_long), so no code loses coverage.

### DEDUP / alias duty

- **P5-T1 CONSUMES P1-T16:** resolved. Retitled "Error-correlator routing + handshake/focus gating — EXTENDS P1-T16". Deliverable reuses P1-T16's `TgpErrorCode` + `wire_code` + `encode_error` (no redefinition, no new `error.rs`); P5-T1 adds only the `ErrorCorr`/`TgpError` correlator types, the `emit_error` formatter, and (via P5-T2) the `should_emit` handshake/focus gating. Added a binding DEDUP note + depends-on `P1-T16 (consumed not redefined)`. The closed-set round-trip is co-owned (G12.3) and re-asserted here as a regression guard only.
- **P5-T25 REUSES P1-T17 `encode_ack`:** resolved. Retitled "`max_backchannel_bytes` enforcement on the ack/error/event queue — EXTENDS P1-T17". Deliverable now lists the P1-T17 `encode_ack` behaviors as "Reused from P1-T17 (not re-specified)" and scopes the **new** work to `max_backchannel_bytes` enforcement on the shared reply queue + `Term::backchannel_pending(tok)`. Added a binding DEDUP note + depends-on `P1-T17 (reused not redefined)`. The retained ack tests are regression guards confirming the backchannel cap does not break the P1-T17 ack path.

### Coverage non-regression

Every in-scope error code keeps ≥1 concrete test: the 24 REACHABLE codes via their ingress reject sites (unchanged or strengthened — `unknown_op` reachable in P4 via the closed-anim-verb locus, the other 23 reachable in P1–P5); the 2 RESERVED codes (`msg_too_large`, `denied`) via P5-T1's closed-set round-trip (`error_code_wire_strings_closed_set` + `reserved_codes_round_trip_only`) — `denied` moved from 5 now-unsound positive sites to the round-trip (parity with `msg_too_large`), no net code-coverage loss. Every advertised cap, header field, lifecycle event, and framing-robustness detail retains its existing concrete test; P5-T3 gained `private_maps_no_existence_oracle` (new `bad_ref` no-oracle assertion) and P5-T1 gained `reserved_codes_round_trip_only`.

### Declined / not-actionable

- None. All six review issues (I-1..I-6), all four G11(a)/(b)/(c)/(d) consequences, all four G12.1/G12.2/G12.3/G12.4 rulings, and both DEDUP duties are actionable and applied in-file.
